Umbral Wolves receive a $+25\%$ attack bonus when attacking from an adjacent flanking tile.
Players must anchor their formation using *Lux Barrio* to prevent fragile party members from
being isolated.
====

== 4. Tactical Grid Map Architecture

Combat within the Whispering Woods utilizes dense environmental obstacles and restrictive
chokepoints:

[source,text]
----
  [0]   [1]   [2]   [3]   [4]   [5]   [6]   [7]
┌─────┬─────┬─────┬─────┬─────┬─────┬─────┬─────┐
│ TREE│ TREE│     │PATH │PATH │     │ TREE│ TREE│ [0] North -> Sacyran Marches
├─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┤
│ TREE│BRIER│     │     │WOLF1│     │BRIER│ TREE│ [1]
├─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┤
│     │     │ROCK │     │     │WOLF2│     │     │ [2] Chokepoint Corridor
├─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┤
│PATH │PATH │     │HERO1│HERO2│     │PATH │PATH │ [3] Player Vanguard Deployment
├─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┼─────┤
│ TREE│     │SHRN │PATH │PATH │     │     │ TREE│ [4] South -> Dio Village
└─────┴─────┴─────┴─────┴─────┴─────┴─────┴─────┘
----

=== Environmental Grid Invariants
1. **Tree & Granite Rock Obstacles**: Completely block line-of-sight and unit transit.
2. **Dense Brier Patches**: Inflict $2.0\times$ movement cost and $-10$ HP per step for non-flying units.
3. **Forester's Shrine (`SHRN`)**: Standing adjacent to the shrine grants $+15\%$ Lux warding efficiency.

== Chapter 11: Realm Chronicle: Dio Village

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/realms/dio_village.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview & Pastoral Heritage

**Dio Village** is a pastoral woodland settlement situated in the southwestern valley frontier of the
**Kingdom of Sacyra**. Functioning as the physical inception of the hero's journey upon awakening
from **The Dreamscape**, Dio Village serves as the terrestrial anchor of Chapter 1, embodying the modern
continuation of ancient Libira's systemic neglect under the 3,000-year dynastic order.

== 1. Socio-Political Role: "The Quiet Complaint" of Modern Sacyra

* **Mirror of Ancient Libira:** Just as ancient Libira suffered a 7-day communication delay and lacked
  imperial relay outposts, modern Dio Village is neglected under the "Joint Realm, Separate Kingdoms"
  bureaucracy. As Grandma Magolia sighs in Map 021: _"This town used to be full of life. *sighs*
  The kingdom doesn't pay much attention to this place anymore..."_.
* **Economic Fragility & Class Exploitation:** While the Capital enjoys insulated peace, Dio Village
  faces monster outbreaks near the timber lines. **Eric the Lumberjack** faces ruin because monsters block
  the woods, while the predatory **St. Baron** exploits commoners through exorbitant rents and usury.
* **Gareth Ro-enheart's Shadow:** Dio Village is where Commander Gareth Ro-enheart placed his son Elijah
  under Grandma Magolia's care before his mysterious disappearance on Elijah's 10th birthday.

== 2. Key Landmarks & Dual-Perspective Quest Hubs

* **Grandma Magolia's Cottage (Maps 020 & 021):** The warm stone hearth where the protagonist awakens.
  Contains the upstairs chest housing **Grandma's Watch** (activating the Layer 0 monotonic clock).
  - *Elijah's Approach:* Hesitates out of filial respect and family etiquette.
  - *Katrina's Approach:* Seeks the will immediately to escape the dead-end town.
* **Eric's Timber Yard & Test of Honesty (Map 028):** Filling Eric's rent pot shifts Moral Sway positively
  and rewards the **Ring of Fire**, enabling flame-weave relic embellishments.
* **Monica's Mystic Grove & Lyri's Offering (Maps 023, 027, 029):** Former Royal Battlemage Monica guards
  the memorial totem of fallen soldiers. Stopping the bandit kid and buying an offering potion for **Lyri,
  the Goddess of Peace**, attunes the relics to arcane resonance and awards the **Book of Monsters**.
* **The St. Baron's Estate & John the Thief (Maps 030 & 031):** Deciding whether to aid **John the Thief**
  in robbing the corrupt baron versus upholding feudal property law shapes the penetration or bastion
  embellishments of the relics.

== 3. Mechanical Role in the C11 Simulation Engine

* **Engine Enumeration:** Registered in `include/sim/sim_types.h` as `LOC_DIO_VILLAGE`.
* **Sanctuary Province:** Zero hostile vigor during standard state; permits inventory management,
  dialogue interactions, moral compass calibration, and Layer 0 monotonic time passage.
* **Terrestrial Lockout:** Gated throughout Chapter 1, preventing return to The Dreamscape until
  the acquisition and awakening of **The Dream Compass**.

== Chapter 12: Realm Chronicle: The Dreamscape

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/realms/the_dreamscape.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview & Metaphysical Essence

**The Dreamscape** (also known as the Astral Substrate or Room of Sages) is an ethereal plane
of consciousness, ancestral memory, and timeless trials existing between waking reality and slumber.
Grounded in the recovered 2017–2018 demo script (Maps 006, 008, 011, 041, 042) and verified in the
ISO C11 engine architecture as `LOC_THE_DREAMSCAPE`, it serves as the cosmological origin point
where the protagonist awakens before entering the temporal Kingdom of Sacyra.

== 1. Historical & Metaphysical Provenance

* **The Coma of the First King:** Following the inaugural Festival of Peoples 3,000 years ago,
  the First King collapsed into a coma. His spirit was found fully lucid and awake inside The Dreamscape.
  While Prince Mi-ren and Prince Casatti sustained projections for ten festivals, the 11th Festival
  tampering by Usuru snapped the ethereal tether, permanently sealing the First King's soul here.
* **The Annual Thinning of the Veil:** For one week each year—coinciding with the Festival of Heroes—the
  boundary ("the Veil") separating the temporal world from The Dreamscape thins to gossamer, allowing
  mortals to project desires across the threshold.
* **The Three Laws of Crossing the Veil:**
  1. *The Mirror of Intent:* The ether reflects mortal intent. Pure devotion projects harmony; terror,
     spite, or megalomania (such as King Jihad's treason) inverts the conduit into waking nightmares.
  2. *The Severed Tether:* Sustaining a long bridge across the veil carries fatal risk. Violent
     interruption snaps spiritual tethers, marooning consciousness permanently.
  3. *The Dream Eater Infiltration:* Tearing or weakening the veil attracts **Dream Eaters**—parasitic
     entities native to the deep astral voids that devour mortal memories, dreams, and ancestral records.

== 2. Mechanical Integration in the C11 Engine

* **Engine Enumeration:** Registered in `include/sim/sim_types.h` as `LOC_THE_DREAMSCAPE`.
* **Opening Trial:** The protagonist awakens in the Hall of Sages with **Chrono the Sage**, attuning
  to class archetype stones and testing `Lux Barrio` and `Cuttatio Tenebris` against Dream Eaters before
  Chrono's command (*"AWAKEN!"*) transports consciousness to Grandma Magolia's hearth in Dio Village.
* **Terrestrial Gating:** Throughout Chapter 1 (the demo), return to The Dreamscape is barred, anchoring
  the player in terrestrial human stakes.
* **The Dream Compass & Nocturnal Phasing:** Upgraded from Grandma's Watch, the Dream Compass allows
  entry to The Dreamscape exclusively during nocturnal hours (`20:00 <= hour || hour <= 04:00`).
* **Tactical LeTBS Environments:** Comprises floating platform grids, shifting crystal leylines,
  and astral mechanisms that restore MP while exposing units to planar shear.

== Chapter 13: High School Inception & TRPG Genesis

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/dev_logs/2026/10/lore_and_design/devlog_002_the_sacyra_chronicles_and_trpg_vision.adoc`

[abstract]
== Abstract

This developer log chronicles the foundational provenance and overarching creative vision
of *Tenebris and Lux*, rooted in its 2014–2018 high school inception as the premier game project
of CreativeWorkzStudio. It documents the elimination of placeholder factions in favor of the canonical
**Kingdom of Sacyra**, the introduction of dual protagonists **Elijah Ro-enheart** and **Katrina Jaren**,
and the technical specification of the game's true genre: a **Tactical Strategy RPG (TRPG)** fusing
grid-based tactical maneuvering with cinematic traditional side-scroll / action-based combat resolution.

== 1. Historical Provenance: The 2014–2018 High School Genesis

Before CreativeWorkzStudio evolved into a multifaceted publishing house, *Tenebris and Lux* was its
very first major game in active development.

* **The RPG Maker Era (2014–2018)**: Conceived while the author was in high school learning game design
  and programming. The initial iterations were built using the default offerings of RPG Maker, exploring
  community plugins, and crafting characters through its Character Generator.
* **The Lost Assets & The Blank Canvas**: With no legacy digital assets surviving from that era, the
  project stands not as a nostalgic port, but as a complete ground-up rebirth—realizing the original
  creative vision with the systems engineering maturity, mathematical rigor, and architectural discipline
  of modern custom engine design.

== 2. Narrative Realignment: The Kingdom of Sacyra

During early prototype scaffolding, generic placeholder factions ("The Solar Order" and "The Umbral Covenant")
were inserted to test basic moral sway logic. On October 26, 2026, the creator firmly realigned the canon,
stripping away these synthetic factions to reveal the true world:

=== 1. The Kingdom of Sacyra (`/sɔːˈkiːrə/`)

* The setting is the historic **Kingdom of Sacyra** (pronounced *"saw-kee-ruh"*).
* Sacyra is a structured, chivalric realm anchored by three distinct socio-political pillars:
  1. **The Crown & Royal Guard**: Guardians of legal order, national defense, and the ancestral throne.
  2. **The Noble Houses & Royal Court**: High aristocracy governing regional fiefs and vying for political dominance on the royal council.
  3. **The Adventurers' Guild**: A pragmatic, frontier-based brotherhood operating beyond courtly vanity, answering commoner monster distress calls and exploring untamed ruins.
* **The Existential Threat**: The ancient sovereign **The Dark Lord** breaking through the Abyssal Vaults, threatening to extinguish all mortal agency.

=== 2. The Dual Protagonists

Rather than a generic lone wanderer, the narrative is anchored by two contrasting protagonists whose
fates, lineages, and martial philosophies intertwine:

[cols="1,2,2", options="header"]
|===
| Character | Elijah Ro-enheart | Lady Katrina Jaren
| **Social Origin** | Military Lineage (Common/Barracks Nobility) | High Aristocracy (House Jaren)
| **Family Heritage** | Son of the former Head of the Royal Guard | Daughter of an influential noble house on the Royal Court
| **Internal Desire** | Living up to his father's legendary military legacy while discovering what true honor demands | Escaping stifling aristocratic court expectations to join the local **Adventurers' Guild**
| **Relic Synergy** | **The Shield of Lux** (Sanctuary, Warding, Formation Hold) | **The Sword of Tenebris** (Severance, Mist-Veil, Critical Strike)
| **TRPG Doctrine** | Frontline heavy vanguard; zone-of-control denial; ally interception | Agile outrider skirmisher; bypassing enemy lines; flanking assassin
|===

== 3. Ludological Evolution: The Tactical Strategy RPG (TRPG)

The game's true mechanical identity is a **Tactical Strategy RPG (TRPG)**. This fundamentally informs
how the simulation engine and presentation viewports are being constructed:

=== 1. The Dual-Layer Combat Loop

The combat loop operates across two synchronized phases:

1. **Strategic Grid Phase**:
   - The battle takes place on a discrete 2D grid representing castles, ramparts, thickets, and void-blighted frontier passes.
   - Units position based on movement ranges, terrain defense bonuses, directional facing (Front, Flank, Rear), and Zone-of-Control (ZOC) restrictions.
2. **Cinematic Combat Resolution (Side-View Action)**:
   - When a unit initiates combat against an enemy target, the game transitions into a dynamic **2D traditional side-scroll / action-based clash**.
   - Attacks play out with timed player agency: precision blocks with the Shield of Lux, poise-breaking shield bashes, and devastating severance slashes with the Sword of Tenebris.
   - Upon duel conclusion, remaining unit health, stagger, and relic resonance seamlessly persist back to the tactical grid.

=== 2. Architectural Implications for the C Engine

Knowing this was originally an RPG Maker project gives us clear architectural directives:
* **Decoupled Data Architecture**: Static definitions (`Units`, `Skills`, `Equipment`, `TerrainTypes`) must exist as clean data tables distinct from session state (`player_state_t`, `grid_t`).
* **Deterministic Event Pipeline**: Interactive map features (gates, switches, dialog triggers) will follow a clean, reentrant command interpreter model.
* **Zero Engine Bloat**: By building this custom in C11 rather than relying on bloated runtimes, our TRPG will maintain sub-millisecond turn updates, instantaneous scene transitions, and absolute softlock immunity.

== 4. Next Milestone Roadmap

1. **Phase 1 (Complete)**: Established OmniCode 5-block layering, Layer 0 utilities, CTest verification suite, and canonical documentation.
2. **Phase 2 (Active)**: Expand simulation state to model dual protagonists (Elijah & Katrina), party data structures, and the foundational 2D tactical grid.
3. **Phase 3**: Implement the cinematic side-scroll combat transition in the presentation layer.
4. **Phase 4**: Expand the tactical campaign across the provinces of Sacyra to confront the vanguard of The Dark Lord.

= Volume II: Dramatis Personae & Dual Protagonist Dynamics

Comprehensive character dossiers, founding princes, dual protagonist synergies, and regional faction leaders.

== Chapter 14: Dual Protagonist Dynamics & Tri-Perspective Architecture

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/characters/protagonists/dual_protagonist_dynamics.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

The narrative core and mechanical heartbeat of *Tenebris and Lux* is the interplay between
**Elijah Ro-enheart** and **Lady Katrina Jaren**, complemented by the optional overarching perspective
of the customizable **Bearer** (`<chosen_name>`). Contrasting in social caste, philosophical outlook,
and combat rhythm, their evolving partnership mirrors the cosmic relationship between Light and Darkness.

== 1. Thematic & Social Contrast Matrix

[cols="1,2,2", options="header"]
|===
| Dimension | Elijah Ro-enheart | Katrina Jaren

| **Social Caste**
| Military Lineage / Son of the Guard Head
| High Aristocracy / House Jaren

| **Institutional Allegiance**
| Royal Army (Structure, Law, Duty)
| Adventurers' Guild Aspirant (Freedom, Merit)

| **Relic Affinity**
| **The Shield of Lux** (Defense, Preservation)
| **The Sword of Tenebris** (Offense, Severance)

| **Thematic Echo**
| **Usuru** (The Frontier Wound / Redemptive Grace)
| **Prince Casatti** (The Renouncing Blade / Truth)

| **Moral Trajectory**
| From rigid obedience to moral conscience
| From cynical detachment to noble sacrifice
|===

== 2. Interpersonal Chemistry & Narrative Progression

Elijah and Katrina embody the classic dramatic friction between an earnest, rule-following guardian
and an irreverent, sharp-witted rogue. Where Elijah pauses to consult protocol, Katrina acts impulsively,
cutting through bureaucratic red tape with sarcastic practicality.

=== Narrative Evolution Across the Campaign
1. **Act I (The Provincial Divide):** Initial friction in Dio Village. Katrina mocks Elijah's military formality;
   Elijah cautions against Katrina's cynical disregard for property and propriety.
2. **Act I Climax (The Capital Conspiracy):** Sacyra Castle. Katrina's insight into aristocratic deception
   exposes King Jihad's treason. Defeating Jihad reveals his possession by The Dark Lord's psychic remnant.
3. **Act II (The Sovereign Eclipse):** Total metaphysical synchronization. Facing **The Dark Lord** at the
   cosmic threshold, the heroes overcome individual biases, passing relics fluidly between each other to
   unlock the **Stance of Equilibrium** (*Duo-Loca Bashin n Slash*).

== 3. The Tri-Perspective Secret Chronicler Architecture

When a player selects a custom name via the engine's `name <str>` command, the game decouples the relics
from fixed protagonists:

* **The Third Eye:** The Custom Bearer acts as an emergent champion guided by **Chrono the Sage** in
  The Dreamscape, holding **The Dream Compass**.
* **Synthesizing Both Tales:** While playing both Elijah and Katrina unlocks the canonical **True Ending**,
  the Custom Bearer performs the "double work" along the `[-100, +100]` Moral Sway axis, catching fragments
  of both investigations to unlock the **Secret Chronicler True Ending**.

== 4. Tactical Synergy on the TRPG Battlefield

1. **The Phalanx Pivot:** Elijah pins enemy vanguards with his Shield of Lux Zone of Control (ZOC) aura.
   Katrina uses high mobility to circle the battle line and execute flanking criticals.
2. **Warded Severance:** Katrina lures high-threat enemies into charging toward her, stepping back onto
   Elijah's tile perimeter as Elijah executes a staggering `Lux Barrio` shield deflection.
3. **The Stance of Equilibrium:** In moments of ultimate synchronization, both relic resonance counters
   combine, unlocking unified abilities where radiant starlight and abyssal void strike in a single coordinated turn.

== Chapter 15: Elijah Ro-enheart: Vanguard of Lux

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/characters/protagonists/elijah_ro_enheart.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

**Elijah Ro-enheart** is the frontline heavy vanguard anchor of *Tenebris and Lux*
and the initial bearer of the **Shield of Lux**. Steeped in the military traditions
of the Royal Guard yet haunted by the unexplained disappearance of his father,
Elijah represents the struggle between rigid institutional duty and personal honor.

== 1. Identity & Heritage

* **Full Name**: Elijah Ro-enheart
* **Title / Rank**: Cadet Vanguard, Sacyran Royal Defense Corps
* **Affiliation**: Kingdom of Sacyra Royal Army; House Ro-enheart
* **Family Lineage**:
  - **Father**: Commander Gareth Ro-enheart, legendary former Head of the
    Royal Guard, celebrated throughout Sacyra for holding the Sunken Citadel.
    Gareth vanished under mysterious crown orders following Elijah's
    **10th birthday**, leaving behind unresolved questions and immense
    expectations.
  - **Grandmother**: **Magolia Ro-enheart** ("Grandma Magolia"), a respected
    matriarch residing in the Upper Ward of Dio Village, guardian of the
    sacred family timepiece (*Grandma's Watch*).
* **Visual Profile**: Broad-shouldered, athletic build, steel-blue eyes, and
  cropped dark hair. Wears fitted silver-steel infantry plate over deep navy
  quilted gambeson, marked with the defensive crest of the Ro-enhearts.

== 2. Psychological Profile & Demeanor

Elijah is earnest, polite, disciplined, and deeply considerate. While raised
within the hierarchy of military discipline, he possesses an intrinsic moral
compass that values the safety of innocent civilians above abstract royal decrees.

=== Canonical Voice & Dialogue (2017–2018 RPG Maker Origin)
* *Respect for Elders & Prudence*:
  [quote, Elijah, Dio Village - Grandma's House]
  "I shouldn't go in this chest. I need to talk to Grandma first. She's very
  particular about her things."
* *The Father Mystery*:
  [quote, Elijah, Opening Quest]
  "Maybe it is something about dad! I haven't seen him since my 10th birthday.
  This might be my chance to get answers!"
* *Civic Duty & Moral Conscience*:
  [quote, Elijah, Lower Village Confrontation]
  "I can't believe that I am doing this... the baron is the richest man in the
  village... but he's been exploiting the town! This is for the good!"

== 3. Martial Doctrine & TRPG Grid Mechanics

Elijah functions as the defensive bulwark of the tactical party, controlling
chokepoints and mitigating damage for allied skirmishers.

[cols="1,2", options="header"]
|===
| Dimension | Specification
| **TRPG Grid Role** | Frontline Heavy Vanguard / Fortress Anchor
| **Movement Budget** | 3 Cells (Medium-Heavy Infantry)
| **Zone of Control (ZOC)**| Projects heavy ZOC tiles, halting enemy sprint moves
| **Primary Relic** | **The Shield of Lux** (Resonance `[0, 100]`)
| **Tactical Signature** | `Warding Aura`: Projects a 2-cell radius barrier dome
| **Poise & Knockback** | Immune to 1-cell forced displacement; absorbs shock
|===

=== Tactical Synergies
1. **Bottleneck Holding**: Elijah anchors narrow corridors, absorbing melee
   punishment while building Lux resonance via successful deflections.
2. **Aegis Interception**: Steps into adjacent cells to intercept lethal strikes
   aimed at fragile skirmishers or casters (e.g., Katrina Jaren).

== Chapter 16: Lady Katrina Jaren: The Outrider Blade

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/characters/protagonists/katrina_jaren.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

**Lady Katrina Jaren** is the high-mobility skirmisher of *Tenebris and Lux*
and the initial wielder of the **Sword of Tenebris**. Born to high aristocracy
yet repulsed by the deceit and stagnation of the Royal Court, Katrina seeks
autonomy within the meritocratic ranks of the Adventurers' Guild.

== 1. Identity & Heritage

* **Full Name**: Lady Katrina Jaren
* **Title / Standing**: Noble Court Aspirant (Estranged); Guild Novice
* **Affiliation**: House Jaren (Eastern Aristocracy); Adventurers' Guild
* **Family Lineage**:
  - **House Jaren**: One of the wealthiest noble lineages in Sacyra, controlling
    lucrative vineyard estates and mountain silver mines. They hold significant
    patronage leverage on the Royal Court.
  - **Kinship with Dio Village**: Despite her family's high status in the
    capital, Katrina spent formative rebellious seasons under the watchful eye
    of Grandma Magolia in Dio Village, seeking respite from court etiquette.
* **Visual Profile**: Agile, athletic build, sharp amber eyes, and unbound
  chestnut hair tied in a practical combat braid. Clad in tailored lightweight
  leather arming doublets over noble-weave tunic, with rapier and long-dagger
  scabbards balanced for rapid unsheathing.

== 2. Psychological Profile & Demeanor

Katrina is quick-witted, irreverent, opportunistic, and intensely independent.
She detests empty aristocratic pretension and speaks with an unvarnished,
sarcastic candor that often unnerves formal soldiers like Elijah. Beneath her
cynical exterior lies a fierce loyalty to those she deems genuine.

=== Canonical Voice & Dialogue (2017–2018 RPG Maker Origin)
* *Impatience with Stagnation & Cynicism*:
  [quote, Katrina, Dio Village - Grandma's House]
  "The will. I love you gran, but a girl gotta get out of this dead-end town.
  Batty old woman seems to mind that chest... Crazy old woman, that will is mine!"
* *Pragmatic Opportunism*:
  [quote, Katrina, The Sword Shrine]
  "Free sword for your girl~!"
* *Fiery Independence & Temper*:
  [quote, Katrina, Monica's Grove]
  "LISTEN HERE YOU OLD-! What is wrong with you... Gran, I'm not your errand
  girl! This is YOUR friend."
* *Reluctant Heroism*:
  [quote, Katrina, Lower Village Heist]
  "Ok... here we go... let's do this. I have to do at least one good thing today."

== 3. Martial Doctrine & TRPG Grid Mechanics

Katrina excels at rapid battlefield repositioning, flanking maneuvers, and
armor-piercing critical strikes.

[cols="1,2", options="header"]
|===
| Dimension | Specification
| **TRPG Grid Role** | High-Mobility Skirmisher / Flanking Striker
| **Movement Budget** | 4–5 Cells (Light Scout / Infiltrator)
| **Zone of Control (ZOC)**| Evasive Infiltration: Ignores basic enemy ZOC delays
| **Primary Relic** | **The Sword of Tenebris** (Edge Sharpness `[0, 100]`)
| **Tactical Signature** | `Phantom Slash`: Teleports behind target cell to strike
| **Evasion & Stealth** | Gains 25% evasion when ending turn on obscured tiles
|===

=== Tactical Synergies
1. **Flanking Tandem**: Moves across enemy flanks while Elijah holds attention,
   landing backstab criticals with the abyssal edge of Tenebris.
2. **Hit-and-Run Decoupling**: Uses remaining move points to disengage after
   striking, retreating behind Elijah's protective barrier dome.

== Chapter 17: The Custom Bearer: Steward of Balance

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/characters/protagonists/custom_bearer.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

The **Custom Bearer** (designated within the simulation engine as `<chosen_name>`)
represents the third sovereign axis of the *Tenebris and Lux* tri-perspective narrative
architecture. Serving as the thematic and ludological pillar of **The Future**, the Custom
Bearer enters the world through the ethereal **Dream Chamber** under the stewardship of
**Chrono**, unencumbered by the heavy generational trauma that binds both Elijah Ro-enheart
and Katrina Jaren.

[quote, Chrono, The Dream Chamber - Awakening of the Relic Bearer]
____
You are not bound to the grief of what was broken, nor the cynicism of what remains.
Touch the stone of your choosing, bearer. The twin relics will answer to your intent,
and the dawn or dusk of Sacyra will bear the shape of your resolve.
____

== 1. Identity & Narrative Axis

* **Canonical Designation**: `<chosen_name>` (User-defined; defaults to `Elijah` if skipped).
* **Narrative Archetype**: The Steward of Balance / The Unwritten Destiny.
* **Thematic Alignment**: **The Future** (Transcending the 3,000-year cycle of inherited trauma).
* **Starting Realm**: **The Dreamscape** (`LOC_THE_DREAMSCAPE`), transitioning into **Dio Village**.
* **Spiritual Lineage**: Spiritual successor to **Prince Mi-ren**, who held the sacred vigil
  and sought institutional equilibrium across the founding era.

[cols="1,2", options="header"]
|===
| Dimension | Characteristic Specification
| **Moral Sway Potential** | Completely unbound: dynamic range `[-100, +100]`.
| **Relic Mastery** | Dual-wield fluency: simultaneous mastery of *Lux Barrio* and *Cuttatio Tenebris*.
| **Dialogue Register** | Inquisitive, perceptive, pragmatic, and unswayed by courtly protocol.
| **Ending Affinity** | Unique accessibility to *The Sovereign Accord* and *The Cycle Shattered*.
|===

== 2. Archetype Stone Awakening (The Four Disciplines)

Upon awakening within the Dream Chamber, the Custom Bearer approaches the four primordial
archetype monoliths under Chrono's observation. Selecting an archetype configures the
starting base parameter profile:

[cols="1,1,2,2", options="header"]
|===
| Archetype Stone | Class Designation | Starting Stat Profile | Tactical Grid Role
| **Aegis Monolith** | *Vanguard* | HP: 480, DEF: 28, ATK: 24, MAT: 18 | Frontline fortress; barrier interception.
| **Shadow Monolith** | *Outrider* | HP: 380, DEF: 18, ATK: 32, MAT: 22 | High mobility; flank severance strikes.
| **Aether Monolith** | *Mystic* | HP: 340, DEF: 16, ATK: 20, MAT: 36 | Elemental burst; area-of-effect zoning.
| **Equilibrium Monolith**| *Steward* | HP: 420, DEF: 22, ATK: 26, MAT: 26 | Balanced hybrid; stance chaining.
|===

[NOTE]
====
Unlike fixed protagonists whose relic bias is established by lineage, the Custom Bearer
accumulates *Resonance Charges* dynamically. Channeling defense builds Lux resonance,
while initiating offensive flanks charges Tenebris edge.
====

== 3. Psychological Dynamic: The Unburdened Gaze

While Elijah sees the world through the lens of military honor and his father's mystery,
and Katrina views society through betrayal and aristocratic corruption, the Custom Bearer
approaches the crisis with empirical curiosity:

* **In Dio Village**: Questions the St. Baron's authority not out of defiance, but by
  exposing the mathematical absurdity of his exorbitant grain tithes.
* **In the Whispering Woods**: Discerns the abnormal migratory patterns of the Umbral Wolves,
  recognizing the abyssal corruption spreading from the Royal Capital.
* **Before King Jihad**: Detects the rhythmic harmonic distortion of *The King's Curse*
  leaking from beneath the High Throne before the royal court notices the monarch's decay.

****
**Thematic Invariant: Inherited Trauma vs. Chosen Destiny**

The Custom Bearer embodies the second clause of the overarching narrative axiom:
*"Trauma is Inherited; Destiny is Chosen."* By refusing to allow ancient grudges between
Libira and Sacyra to dictate their actions, the Custom Bearer can forge alliances that
neither historical faction believed possible.
****

== 4. TRPG Grid Mechanics & Signature Abilities

[cols="1,2", options="header"]
|===
| Dimension | Specification
| **Base Movement** | 4 Tiles (Variable by chosen Archetype Stone: 3 to 5 tiles).
| **Zone of Control** | Dynamic projection based on active combat stance.
| **Signature Stance** | *Harmonic Equilibrium* (`STANCE_EQUILIBRIUM`): $+15\%$ to both DEF and ATK.
| **Ultimate Skill** | *Duo-Loca Bashin n Slash* (Twilight Magic Slash combining Lux and Tenebris).
|===

=== Tactical Synergies
1. **Adaptive Stance Shifting**: Can shift stances without consuming action points once per turn,
   allowing instant transition from defensive warding to aggressive edge severance.
2. **Moral Sway Polarization**: Can align with either light or dark embellishments at the
   Altar of the Twin Relics, or forge the *Equilibrium Crown* to dissolve the planar wound.

== Chapter 18: Prince Mi-ren: Sovereign of the Vigil

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/characters/founders/prince_mi_ren.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

**Prince Mi-ren** was the elder founding prince of the Kingdom of Sacyra during Era I
(The Primordial Dawn & The Accord of Kings). While his younger brother, Prince Casatti,
ventured into the untamed frontier, Mi-ren anchored the capital, established the administrative
institutions of the Crown, and held the legendary **Four-Year Vigil** over his brother's
mysterious slumber. In the narrative triad of *Tenebris and Lux*, Prince Mi-ren represents
the archetype of **The Steward of Order** and the philosophical progenitor of the Custom Bearer.

[quote, Prince Mi-ren, Royal Archive Tablet - The Founding Accord]
____
A wall does not exist to lock men in, but to ensure that what we plant inside may grow
without fear of the winter gale. If my brother must walk the dark wild, then I shall build
a hearth warm enough to guide his spirit home.
____

== 1. Identity & Lineage

* **Full Title**: Prince Mi-ren the Steadfast, First Warden of the High Court, Architect of Sacyra.
* **Dynastic Era**: Era I–II (Founding Epoch, approximately 3,000 Years Before the Main Campaign).
* **Affiliation**: First Royal House of Sacyra; Founder of the Royal Garrison and Courier Corps.
* **Familial Ties**:
  - **Brother**: **Prince Casatti**, the younger outrider prince who walked the Path of Darkness.
  - **Successor Lineage**: His bloodline formed the foundational dynasty of the First 30 Kings,
    eventually degenerating millennia later into the hollow reign of King Jihad.

[cols="1,2", options="header"]
|===
| Dimension | Characteristic Specification
| **Martial Discipline** | Classical Phalanx & Warding Architecture; heavy royal shieldbearer.
| **Metaphysical Affinity** | **The Shield of Lux** (Resonance with warding, preservation, and structure).
| **Thematic Anchor** | Institutional Duty, Self-Sacrifice, Bureaucratic Order.
|===

== 2. Historical Chronicle & The Four-Year Vigil

When the two founding princes completed their training, they faced a philosophical fork:
the untamed frontier required exploration, while the nascent kingdom required permanent
governance and defensible walls.

[cols="1,3", options="header"]
|===
| Event Phase | Historical Deed & Chronicle
| **The Division of Labor** | Mi-ren took responsibility for the Crown, founding the Royal Guard,
  designing the granaries, and codifying Sacyran civil law.
| **The Slumber of Casatti** | When Prince Casatti collapsed into a four-year coma during his
  wilderness campaign, Mi-ren refused to abandon his brother, maintaining a continuous daily vigil
  at his bedside while governing the kingdom through sleepless devotion.
| **The Festival of Peoples**| Established the international gathering in the capital, inviting
  surrounding realms to exchange culture, technology, and mutual peace every spring.
| **The Tragic Rupture** | In the 11th year, when the planar tether snapped beneath the High Throne,
  Mi-ren threw his own life-force into the warding perimeter to shield the capital's populace from
  abyssal incineration.
|===

[NOTE]
====
Prince Mi-ren's great tragedy was his belief that systemic perfection could eliminate human suffering.
By focusing entirely on the capital's defense, his administrative network failed to recognize the
desperation building in frontier outposts like Libira, inadvertently sowing the seeds of Usuru's revolt.
====

== 3. Thematic Mirror: The Custom Bearer & Prince Mi-ren

In the **Past / Present / Future Thematic Triad**, Prince Mi-ren serves as the historical
touchstone for the **Custom Bearer**:

1. **The Burden of Stewardship**: Just as Mi-ren held the kingdom together through sheer discipline,
   the Custom Bearer is called upon to arbitrate between the competing factions of modern Sacyra.
2. **Transcending Institutional Rigidity**: Where Mi-ren prioritized legalistic structure over
   emotional grievance, the Custom Bearer must learn from Mi-ren's failure—listening to the
   "quiet complaints" of commoners in Dio Village and the frontier before they curdle into war.
3. **Equilibrium Mastery**: Historical records indicate that Mi-ren was the first to realize that
   the Shield of Lux could not withstand endless kinetic pressure without venting force through
   the severance of a blade—laying the conceptual groundwork for the *Equilibrium Stance*.

== Chapter 19: Prince Casatti: The Outrider Blade of Truth

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/characters/founders/prince_casatti.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

**Prince Casatti** was the younger founding prince of Sacyra during Era I. Rejecting the
ceremonial comforts of the royal palace, Casatti rode into the frontier wilderness to chart
the uncharted reaches of the continent. Falling into a mysterious **Four-Year Coma** after
contacting an ethereal boundary stone, Casatti awakened with transfigured perception,
wielding the **Sword of Tenebris** as an instrument of decisive severance and unvarnished truth.
In the overarching narrative of *Tenebris and Lux*, Casatti serves as the direct historical
predecessor and spiritual mirror to **Lady Katrina Jaren**.

[quote, Prince Casatti, Fragmented Journal Recovered from the Whispering Caverns]
____
My brother measures safety by the thickness of a parapet. He does not understand that
stone can rot from within while looking immaculate to the sun. There are times when peace
is simply another word for cowardice. When the branch turns gangrenous, you do not bless
it with oil—you strike it from the trunk.
____

== 1. Identity & Provenance

* **Full Title**: Prince Casatti the Swift, The Frontier Ranger, Bearer of the First Edge.
* **Dynastic Era**: Era I–II (Founding Epoch, approximately 3,000 Years Before the Main Campaign).
* **Affiliation**: Royal House of Sacyra; Spiritual Patron of the Outrider Scouts and Adventurers' Guild.
* **Familial Ties**:
  - **Brother**: **Prince Mi-ren**, the elder sovereign who held the capital vigil.
  - **Spiritual Heir**: **Lady Katrina Jaren**, who inherits his martial mindset, defiance of
    aristocratic complacency, and razor-sharp swordsmanship.

[cols="1,2", options="header"]
|===
| Dimension | Characteristic Specification
| **Martial Discipline** | High-mobility skirmishing, critical edge strikes, flank severance.
| **Metaphysical Affinity** | **The Sword of Tenebris** (Resonance with severance, decisive truth, shadow).
| **Thematic Anchor** | Radical Truth, Severing Stagnation, Rejection of Hypocrisy.
|===

== 2. The Four-Year Slumber & The Ethereal Transfiguration

While exploring the frontier passes bordering the ancient realm of Libira, Casatti touched
an anomalous obsidian monolith vibrating with abyssal frequency. The contact thrust him into
a deep suspended animation that lasted four full years:

1. **The Slumber**: For forty-eight months, his body rested unaging in the capital while his
   consciousness traversed the deeper strata of **The Dreamscape**.
2. **The Awakening**: Upon awakening, Casatti no longer viewed darkness as an evil force to be
   feared, but as the quiet, necessary soil from which all life and truth spring.
3. **The Sword of Tenebris**: Crafting the ancestral blade that would become the counterpart
   to the Shield of Lux, Casatti demonstrated that light without darkness produces blinding
   arrogance, while darkness tempered by discipline brings clarity.

[IMPORTANT]
====
Casatti's critique of the royal court's decadence was violently rejected by the conservative
nobles of Sacyra. Frustrated by courtly hypocrisy, he spent his remaining years defending
the frontier peasantry, establishing the traditions later adopted by the **Adventurers' Guild**.
====

== 3. Thematic Mirror: Katrina Jaren & Prince Casatti

The narrative parallels between Prince Casatti and Lady Katrina Jaren are exact:

* **Aristocratic Renunciation**: Just as Casatti abandoned the throne room to live among frontier
  scouts, Katrina abandons her aristocratic seat on the Royal Court to join the commoners of the
  Adventurers' Guild.
* **The Blade of Severance**: Both wield the Sword of Tenebris not to conquer, but to cut through
  political lies, aristocratic complacency, and ideological self-deception.
* **The Present Axis**: In the narrative triad, both embody **The Present**—the urgent necessity
  to take decisive action against immediate injustice rather than hiding behind royal procedure.

== Chapter 20: Usuru the Wanderer: Sovereign of Grief

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/characters/founders/usuru_the_wanderer.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

Before he was feared across the ages as **The Dark Lord**, he was a nameless boy born to an
impoverished family on the arid borders of the **Kingdom of Libira** during Era IV. Known
as the **Three-Day Wanderer** because he could never bear to settle in any settlement for
more than three sunsets, he held an unyielding, reverent devotion to the First King and the
splendor of the Capital. Driven by the "Quiet Complaint" of his neglected homeland and
catastrophically altered by the snapping of the planar tether during the 11th Festival of Peoples,
Usuru chose his name in defiance, crowned himself sovereign of Libira, and declared the
devastating **Seven-Year Great War**.

[quote, Sovereign Usuru, Speech to the Assembly of Libira - Dawn of the Great War]
____
We waited seven days for their riders to tell us if our children were allowed to starve.
We bowed when their golden heralds told us that peace demanded our silence. But when I
reached out to touch the King's light, it burned my skin and sealed our skies. They did
not leave us behind by mistake—they left us behind because we were quiet. Libira will be
quiet no longer.
____

== 1. Identity & Origin

* **Birth Identity**: Nameless Boy of Libira (Born to a frontier shepherd family).
* **Chosen Name**: **Usuru** (Ancient Libiran root meaning *"He Who Remembers What Was Buried"*).
* **Historical Era**: Era IV–V (approximately 3,000 Years Ago, Pre-Dynastic Wave).
* **Titles**: The Three-Day Wanderer; Sovereign of the Red Desert; Vanguard of the Broken Crown;
  later corrupted across the millennia into **The Dark Lord**.
* **Affiliation**: Kingdom of Libira; Supreme Commander of the 6-Power Coalition against Sacyra.

[cols="1,2", options="header"]
|===
| Dimension | Specification
| **Defining Trait** | Restlessness; profound observational empathy curdling into volcanic resentment.
| **Original Motivation**| Yearning to bridge the geographic and communication gulf between Libira and Sacyra.
| **Thematic Axis** | **The Past** (The unhealed frontier wound, mirrored by Elijah Ro-enheart).
|===

== 2. The Wanderer's Pilgrimage & The "Quiet Complaint"

Due to the lack of an outpost relay system, a message sent from the Royal Capital took seven
grueling days of desert riding to reach Libira. While the capital feasted, Libira struggled
against drought, bandit raids, and forgotten infrastructure.

1. **The Three-Day Law**: Growing up, the boy walked the breadth of the continent, staying no more
   than three days in any village. He listened to commoners, blacksmiths, and outriders, cataloging
   the quiet complaints of the forgotten provinces.
2. **The Annual Pilgrimage**: The sole exception to his three-day rule was the annual **Festival of
   Peoples** in the capital, where he stayed an entire week, gazing with awe at the High Throne and
   the First King.
3. **The 11th Year Catastrophe**: During the 11th festival, determined to deliver a petition for a
   Libiran relay road, the boy slipped into the Inner Sanctum beneath the High Throne. Startled by
   guards, he accidentally struck the ritual focus stone. The planar tether snapped, venting primordial
   abyssal energy that sealed the First King in the Dreamscape and violently warped the distance
   between Libira and Sacyra into an impenetrable spatial rift.

[WARNING]
====
Believing the sudden spatial expansion was a deliberate imperial curse cast by the Sacyran court
to permanently exile Libira, the boy's devotion inverted into pure, consuming hatred.
====

== 3. Ascension, War & Metaphysical Corruption

Returning across the warped desert passes, the youth took the name **Usuru**, mounted the dais
of the Libiran assembly hall, and proclaimed the end of subservience:

* **The Crown of Libira**: Libirans, enraged by generations of neglect and royal silence, crowned
  Usuru by unanimous acclamation.
* **The 6-Power Conflagration**: Forging military alliances with the Great Mau Kingdom, Old Sacyra,
  and the Yun-yin Provinces, Usuru ignited the **Seven-Year Great War**.
* **Descent into Ruin**: To match the invulnerable barrier of the *Shield of Lux*, Usuru dipped his
  soul repeatedly into the abyssal breach beneath the battlefield, forging void constructs and
  commanding *Dream Eaters*. By the war's end, his humanity had burned away, leaving behind the
  eternal, brooding sovereign now remembered only as **The Dark Lord**.

== 4. Thematic Mirror: Elijah Ro-enheart & Usuru

Usuru serves as the tragic historical negative to **Elijah Ro-enheart**:

* Both originate from the marginalized periphery (frontier Libira vs. neglected Dio Village).
* Both suffer from the abrupt disappearance of a revered paternal figure (The First King vs. Commander Gareth).
* Where Usuru allowed his frontier grievance to consume him into becoming a monster of vengeance,
  Elijah must learn to acknowledge his family's pain without repeating Usuru's destructive crusade.

== Chapter 21: Chrono: Sage of the Ancient Archives & Dream Guardian

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/characters/sages_and_ancients/chrono.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

**Chrono** is the eternal archival sage and guardian of **The Dream Chamber**
within the ethereal substrate of **The Dreamscape** (`LOC_THE_DREAMSCAPE`).
Witness to the 3,000-year dynastic wave and the catastrophic snapping of the planar
tether during the 11th Festival of Peoples, Chrono serves as the guide, mentor,
and mechanic instructor for the newly awakened Relic Bearer.

[quote, Chrono, The Dream Chamber - Tutorial Trial]
____
Time is neither a line nor a river, child of Sacyra. It is a loom of resonance.
When the First King attempted to hold the veil open, the shuttle broke. What you call
monsters are merely the untethered threads of that unbroken night seeking form.
Raise your ward. Let us see if your spirit remembers how to hold the dawn.
____

== 1. Identity & Provenance

* **Full Designation**: Chrono, Keeper of the Monoliths and Primordial Archives.
* **Affiliation**: Pre-Dynastic Sacyran Order of Scribes; Guardian of the Dream Veil.
* **Sanctuary Domain**: **The Dream Chamber** (Subterranean astral vault within the Dreamscape).
* **Physical Manifestation**: An ethereal figure draped in celestial silver-grey robes lined
  with rotating astrolabe embroidery. His hair shifts between shades of starlight, and his
  eyes glow with muted amber luminescence. He holds the *Codex of Eras*, an unwritten ledger
  of potential futures.

[cols="1,2", options="header"]
|===
| Dimension | Specification
| **Age / Lifespan** | Ageless; active since the dawn of the First Dynasty (>3,000 years).
| **Primary Relic** | **The Chrono Astrolabe** (Attunes pocket dimensions and temporal flow).
| **Tone & Register** | Measured, archaic, compassionate yet unsentimental; philosophical.
| **Mechanical Role** | Archetype Stone Selection, Combat Tutorial Arbiter, Dream Compass Attuner.
|===

== 2. Archival Purpose & Historical Memory

Chrono was present when Prince Mi-ren held his vigil in the capital and when Prince Casatti
fell into the four-year coma. He witnessed Usuru the Wanderer arrive as a nameless pilgrim,
and documented the exact instant the 11th-year ritual collapsed beneath the High Throne.

[IMPORTANT]
====
Chrono does not intervene directly in the mortal wars of Sacyra. The Laws of the Veil
forbid an Archival Sage from taking up arms against the living or the void. His duty is
solely to ensure that the chosen Relic Bearer understands the consequences of moral intent.
====

=== The Three Archival Directives
1. **Preserve the Blueprint of Balance**: Record the deeds of each monarch across the 3,000-year
   dynastic wave, tracking the accumulation of stasis residue beneath the capital.
2. **Attune the Relic Bearer**: Test the newly awakened soul against the *Dream Eaters* to verify
   that they can command both Lux deflection and Tenebris severance without succumbing to madness.
3. **Calibrate The Dream Compass**: Awaken the dormant temporal gearwork within *Grandma's Watch*,
   transforming a rustic heirloom into an instrument capable of navigating nocturnal planar shifts.

== 3. Canonical Voice & Dialogue

* *The Philosophy of Stance*:
  [quote, Chrono, Combat Mechanics Initiation]
  "To wield only Lux is to become a frozen statue, impervious yet inert. To wield only
  Tenebris is to become a devouring blade, sharp yet hollow. True mastery lies in the
  threshold where the two kiss and do not consume one another."

* *Warning of The King's Curse*:
  [quote, Chrono, Midpoint Revelation]
  "King Jihad is not evil, bearer. He is thirsty. And a thirsty man standing over an open
  well of liquid night will drink until his belly bursts and his soul turns black."

* *The Fate of Usuru*:
  [quote, Chrono, The Planar Threshold]
  "Do not despise the Dark Lord. Mourn him. For before he was a sovereign of ruin, he was a
  lonely boy whose quiet complaint went unheard by a court blinded by its own radiance."

== 4. Ludological Role in the TRPG Engine

[cols="1,2", options="header"]
|===
| Gameplay Phase | Systemic Interaction
| **Prologue (Awakening)** | Guides archetype stone interaction; spawns initial Dream Eater combatants.
| **Act I Interludes** | Re-encountered during rest at campfires when the player falls into dream meditation.
| **Act II (Midpoint)** | Deciphers the runes carved into *Grandma's Watch*, unlocking the Dream Compass.
| **Act III (Endgame)** | Unlocks the *Sanctum of Forgotten Memories*, revealing Usuru's uncorrupted dialogue.
|===

****
**Sidebar: Chrono's Codex & Invariant Validation**

Within the C11 simulation engine, Chrono serves as the diagnotic voice of the
subsystem self-audit routines (`core.util.manifest`, `sim.engine.state`). When the engine
performs an invariant check, the telemetry trace reflects Chrono's unyielding standard:
zero heap corruption, bounded moral sway, and deterministic state transitions.
****

== Chapter 22: Grandma Magolia: Matriarch of Dio Village

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/characters/dio_village/grandma_magolia.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

**Grandma Magolia** (Magolia Ro-enheart) is the beloved matriarch of Dio
Village, mother to Commander Gareth Ro-enheart, and grandmother to Elijah
Ro-enheart. Providing warmth, historical perspective, and the family heirloom
(*Grandma's Watch*), Grandma Magolia anchors the heroes to the human heart
of Sacyra.

== 1. Identity & Family Ties

* **Full Name**: Magolia Ro-enheart ("Grandma Magolia")
* **Residence**: Grandma's House (Two-story timber cottage, Dio Upper Ward)
* **Family Lineage**:
  - **Son**: Commander Gareth Ro-enheart (Vanished Head of the Royal Guard)
  - **Grandson**: Elijah Ro-enheart (Bearer of the Shield of Lux)
  - **Surrogate Charge**: Lady Katrina Jaren (who sought haven with Magolia
    during her rebellion against House Jaren court tutors)
  - **Lifelong Friend**: Monica the Mystic (companion since youth)

== 2. Canonical Voice & Dialogue (2017–2018 RPG Maker Origin)

Grandma Magolia speaks with gentle affection, nostalgic wisdom, and quiet
sadness regarding the decline of provincial Sacyra.

* *The Heirloom Quest*:
  [quote, Grandma Magolia, Map 21 / Map 7]
  "Hello sweetie, could you please go upstairs and search in the chest and grab
  what's inside? It's something that has great significance."
* *Reflections on the Kingdom's Neglect*:
  [quote, Grandma Magolia, Map 21 / Map 7]
  "This town used to be full of life. *sighs* The kingdom doesn't pay much
  attention to this place anymore..."
* *Passing Down the Heirloom Watch*:
  [quote, Grandma Magolia, Map 21 / Map 7]
  "Thank you honey. This watch has been in the family for a long time. Oh, how
  it brings back memories of better days. I think you should keep it. It's my
  gift to you. And here's a bit of money. I know that you'll need it more than
  little ol' me."
* *A Lifelong Bond with Monica*:
  [quote, Grandma Magolia, Map 21 / Map 7]
  "Oh me and Monica go way back. She was such a wild child, always getting into
  mischief. You'll love her."

== 3. The Heirloom Timepiece: Grandma's Watch

Grandma Magolia bequeaths **Grandma's Watch** to the party. In the original
2017–2018 RPG Maker project, this item activated the global calendar and
monotonic time engine (Seconds, Minutes, Hours, Days, Weeks, Months, and
Seasons). In the modern C11 architecture, this lore item corresponds directly
to the Layer 0 `time_utils` subsystem, tracking monotonic game ticks and frame
budgets.

== Chapter 23: Monica the Mystic & Goddess Lyri

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/characters/dio_village/monica_and_lyri.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

**Monica the Mystic** (known as *The Sage* in village folklore) and her
elemental familiar **Lyri** reside in an arcane grove on the outskirts of
Upper Dio Village. Sarcastic, sharp-tongued, yet deeply attuned to the metaphysical
currents of Sacyra, Monica is the first local authority to confirm that the
forces of Darkness have re-awakened.

== 1. Identity & Mystical Lineage

* **Name**: Monica (*The Mystic / The Sage*)
* **Companion Familiar**: **Lyri**, an ethereal nature spirit who perceives
  metaphysical fluctuations and whispers warnings into Monica's ear.
* **Residence**: Monica's House & Mystic Grove (Dio Upper Ward)
* **Alliances**: Lifelong confidante of Grandma Magolia; contact of Chrono the Sage.

== 2. Canonical Voice & Demeanor (2017–2018 RPG Maker Origin)

Monica possesses a biting, irreverent wit, greeting newcomers with playful
insults before demonstrating profound arcane knowledge.

* *Blunt First Impressions*:
  [quote, Monica to Elijah, Map 27 / Map 29]
  "And who in the blaze are YOU! ... So you are Magolia's grandson. She made you
  sound more handsome than you are."
* *Clashing with Katrina*:
  [quote, Monica to Katrina, Map 27 / Map 29]
  "So you are Magolia's granddaughter. She made you sound prettier than you are." +
  *Katrina*: "LISTEN HERE YOU OLD-!" +
  *Monica*: "Tis only a joke. Now why have you come here? Do you have some milk?"
* *Lyri's Whispers & The Darkness*:
  [quote, Monica, Map 27 / Map 29]
  "A wise woman she is. Lyri has been whispering in me ear about the forces of
  darkness. I'll help, but first, get that hooligan from the city limits. Not
  only is he preventing folks from leaving, he's also messing with me business."

== 3. Arcane Role in the Campaign

1. **Attunement to the Void**: Through Lyri, Monica senses the breaking of the
   ancient seals on the Abyssal Threshold long before the Royal Court admits it.
2. **The City Limits Obstacle**: Directs the heroes to clear the town boundary
   (confronting the "Fighter Kid" blocking the pass) to prove their tactical
   readiness.
3. **Bridge to the Sages**: Connects Elijah and Katrina with the ancient scholar
   **Chrono**, guiding them toward their destiny with the twin relics.

== Chapter 24: Eric the Lumberjack & The Moral Litmus

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/characters/dio_village/eric_the_lumberjack.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

**Eric** is a former lumberjack residing in the Upper Ward of Dio Village. His
livelihood was shattered when a mysterious abyssal portal manifested in the
timber forests, driving him into poverty. He introduces the heroes to the
**Ring of Fire** and anchors the early **Test of Honesty** moral dilemma.

== 1. Identity & Background

* **Name**: Eric
* **Occupation**: Former Master Woodman / Timberjack (Unemployed due to monsters)
* **Residence**: Eric's Hearth (Upper Dio Village)
* **Economic Situation**: Deeply impoverished, struggling to afford food as
  monster activity blocks access to the logging roads.

== 2. Canonical Voice & Dialogue (2017–2018 RPG Maker Origin)

Eric represents the vulnerable provincial working class bearing the brunt of
unnatural cosmic incursions.

* *The Struggle of Provincial Labor*:
  [quote, Eric, Map 28]
  "We've been seeing a lot of monsters appear near the woods recently. I wonder
  what's that all about. And with jobs being tight, money is hard to come by."
* *The Story of the Portal & The Ring of Fire*:
  [quote, Eric, Map 28]
  "Thank you. Thank you so much, here I believe that this will be more useful to
  you than me. The name is Eric and that ring has a story. I used to be a
  lumberjack until that weird portal appeared. I found this ring and held on to
  it ever since. I'm sure that ring's fire power will come in handy if you plan
  on leaving the village."

== 3. The Test of Honesty & Moral Continuum

Eric's home contains a famous interactive vignette from the 2017–2018 project:
the **Self-Aware Pot**.

* If the player attempts to loot Eric's shelf pot, the pot speaks directly to the
  player:
  [quote, Self-Aware Pot, Map 28]
  "What kind of game do you think this is! You can't just steal whatever you
  want! How rude!"
* Taking the 10 Gold increments the internal `Moral (Evil)` counter, while
  gifting charity to Eric increments `Moral (Good)`. This directly fed the
  original RPG Maker `Morality Meter` and served as the design prototype for
  the modern C11 engine's `moral_sway` continuum (`[-100, +100]`).

== Chapter 25: John the Thief & Outlaw Resistance

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/characters/dio_village/john_the_thief.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

**John the Thief** is an underworld operative and smuggler residing in the
back alleys of Dio Village's Lower Ward. Cynical and gruff, John nonetheless
acts as an unofficial protector of the poor, redistributing goods and offering
clandestine sidequests to heroes willing to bypass the law.

== 1. Identity & Underworld Standing

* **Name**: John (*"John the Thief"*)
* **Occupation**: Smuggler, Fence, and Independent Rogue Operative
* **Territory**: Lower Ward Alleys & Shopping District
* **Philosophy**: Pragmatic survivalism; contempt for aristocratic landlords.

== 2. Canonical Voice & Dialogue (2017–2018 RPG Maker Origin)

John initially meets outsiders with hostility, softening only when heroes
demonstrate that they care about the common people.

* *Gruff Rebuff*:
  [quote, John, Map 31]
  "What do you want kid! Can't you see that I'm busy!"
* *Recognizing Civic Virtue*:
  [quote, John, Map 31]
  "So you're the one that's helping the little people of this village out. I have
  a little task for you..."

== 3. Quest & Mechanics Role

John the Thief introduces the **Stolen Goods** tactical retrieval mechanics.
In the 2017–2018 LeTBS combat engine, recovered bandit contraband appeared as
interactive grid items with dedicated `Pick Up` and `Push` commands. John serves
as the quest receiver who turns recovered goods into vital supplies and gold.

== Chapter 26: The St. Baron & Feudal Exploitation

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/characters/dio_village/st_baron.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

**The St. Baron** is the wealthiest aristocrat and dominant landowner of Dio
Village. Residing in a secluded manor in the Lower Ward, the St. Baron uses
exorbitant rent, grain hoarding, and monopolies to subjugate the impoverished
citizens, serving as a primary symbol of noble corruption outside the capital.

== 1. Identity & Standing

* **Title**: The St. Baron of Dio Province
* **Residence**: The St. Baron's Manor (Lower Dio Village)
* **Status**: High provincial gentry; tax collector for the eastern march
* **Reputation**: Ruthless, miserly, and widely despised by the working poor.

== 2. Socio-Economic Conflict in Dio Lower Ward

While the Upper Ward remains pastoral and self-sufficient, the Lower Ward
suffers under the St. Baron's heavy-handed economic extortion. By controlling
grain warehouses and money-lending stalls, the Baron has forced shopkeepers and
laborers into debt bondage.

* *The Hero's Moral Justification (Elijah)*:
  [quote, Elijah, Lower Village Infiltration]
  "I can't believe that I am doing this... the baron is the richest man in the
  village... but he's been using the town! This is for the good!"
* *The Rogue's Determination (Katrina)*:
  [quote, Katrina, Lower Village Infiltration]
  "Ok... here we go... let's do this. I have to do at least one good thing today."

== 3. Narrative & Quest Role

The St. Baron represents the mundane human corruption that flourishes when the
Crown neglects its borders. Infiltrating the St. Baron's estate to recover stolen
resources for the village provides the player with an early non-combat tactical
scenario, forcing Elijah to break legal statutes for the higher moral good.

== Chapter 27: High Priest Roté & The Royal Omens

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/characters/kingdom_and_court/sir_rote.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

**Sir Roté** is a veteran scout, senior knight, and herald of the Sacyran
Royal Guard. His urgent, breathless arrival at Sacyra Castle serves as the
opening narrative incitement of the game, delivering news of the rising island,
the unnatural storm, and the return of the primordial Darkness.

== 1. Identity & Standing

* **Full Name**: Sir Roté of the Royal Guard
* **Rank / Office**: Senior Outrider Captain / First Scout of the Realm
* **Affiliation**: Kingdom of Sacyra Royal Army; The Royal Guard
* **Visual Demeanor**: Weather-worn plate mail spattered with mud from frantic
  galloping, exhausted, earnest, and deeply devoted to the Crown.

== 2. Canonical Voice & Dialogue (2017–2018 RPG Maker Origin)

Sir Roté represents the perspective of the loyal rank-and-file soldier who
believes unquestioningly in his monarch's ability to save the realm.

* *Observation at the Frontier (Start-Up / Castle Gates)*:
  [quote, Roté, Map 2 & Map 3 / Map 8]
  "The king... huff... huff... huff... he must know immediately! And to think...
  The storm... The rising island... All of these events before the Festival of
  Heroes. The king will know how to stop this, he must know..."
* *The Urgent Report to King Jihad*:
  [quote, Roté, Throne Room - Map 4 / Map 9]
  "MY KING! It's here! The darkness... it has returned!"
* *The Uneasy Hesitation*:
  [quote, Roté, Hearing King Jihad's Gag Order]
  "Y... Y... Yes my king..."

== 3. Narrative Role & Dynamic

Sir Roté serves as a tragic mirror to Elijah Ro-enheart. Both men revere the
oath of the Royal Guard. However, while Roté suppresses his growing unease
when King Jihad issues a gag order preventing the people from knowing about the
approaching Darkness, Elijah ultimately chooses conscience over blind obedience.
Later in the campaign, Roté's internal conflict plays a pivotal role in enabling
Elijah and Katrina to infiltrate the castle archives.

== Chapter 28: King Jihad & The Treasonous Ambition

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/characters/kingdom_and_court/king_jihad.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

**King Jihad** (referenced in ancient annals as *D. Jihad*) is the reigning
monarch of the **Kingdom of Sacyra**. Outwardly a dignified sovereign presiding
over the chivalric traditions and the **Festival of Heroes**, King Jihad secretly
harbors a perilous ambition: to seize and harness the forbidden abyssal power of
the returning **Dark Lord** to establish absolute dominion over the realm.

== 1. Identity & Sovereign Office

* **Full Name**: King Jihad of Sacyra (Monarchical Title: *D. Jihad*)
* **Office**: Crown Sovereign, King of Sacyra, High Commander of the Realm
* **Seat of Power**: The High Citadel of Sacyra Castle
* **Political Standing**: Balances the aristocratic Noble Houses against the
  meritocratic Adventurers' Guild while commanding the elite Royal Guard.

== 2. The Grotesque Inversion of the Founding Dynasty

King Jihad stands as the exact moral and structural inverse of the First King and Prince Mi-ren:

[cols="1,2,2", options="header"]
|===
| Dimension | The Founding Dynasty (First King & Mi-ren) | The Modern Inversion (King Jihad)

| **State of the Monarch**
| Physical body comatose/dead during festival week, while his **spirit remained pure and lucid** in The Dreamscape to guide the realm.
| Physical body healthy upon the throne, while his **heart is thoroughly corrupted** by greed for void power.

| **Purpose of Secrecy**
| Concealed the First King's passing out of filial duty and preservation until teenage Mi-ren officially came of age.
| Imposes a gag order (`"don't tell the people"`) so heroes do not interfere before Jihad can usurp void power for himself.

| **Relation to Prophecy**
| Forged Lux and Tenebris to enact justice, protect life, and seek forgiveness.
| Covets the **Prophecy of Kings**, seeking to hijack void supremacy without understanding that Lux dissipates in a corrupt heart.
|===

== 3. "The King's Curse" & Abyssal Possession

* **The Planar Hazard:** For 3,000 years, Sacyran monarchs were exposed to residual spiritual shear
  from the open planar rift beneath the High Throne dias, left when Usuru shattered the First King's
  stasis ritual.
* **The Fatal Gamble:** Coveting the Dark Lord's power, King Jihad intentionally opened the conduit
  beneath his throne prior to the Festival of Heroes. Because the ethereal medium mirrors mortal intent,
  Jihad's arrogance inverted the flow, allowing a dormant remnant of Usuru's consciousness (**The Dark Lord**)
  to possess him.
* **Act I Midpoint Climax:** Defeating King Jihad at Sacyra Castle exposes this possession, revealing that
  the true threat lies deep in the subterranean Abyssal Vaults where the ancient soul of Usuru still waits.

== 4. Canonical Voice & Dialogue (2017–2018 RPG Maker Origin)

* *The Secret Soliloquy:*
  [quote, D. Jihad, High Throne Room]
  "The fools... that power... that wicked power! Soon I will rule over this land,
  and not even the Dark Lord will have power over me!"
* *Suppressing the Truth:*
  [quote, King Jihad to Sir Roté]
  "Thank you Roté, what would I do without your careful watch... Gather up the
  guard! We need to prepare! Darkness will soon return to this land. And don't
  tell the people of this discovery... You all are dismissed."

== 5. Socio-Political Role in the Campaign

1. **State Censorship:** Enforces an information blackout along the border, ignoring Sir Roté's warnings.
2. **Gareth's Disappearance:** Dispatched Commander Gareth Ro-enheart on a suicide mission into the
   Abyssal Threshold on Elijah's 10th birthday to scout void portals, leaving the Ro-enhearts fractured.
3. **The Catalyst for Rebellion:** When Elijah and Katrina expose King Jihad's corruption, Elijah is forced
   to choose between his sworn oath to the throne and his true moral duty to the living people of Sacyra.

== Chapter 29: Commander Gareth Ro-enheart & The Vanished Guard

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/characters/kingdom_and_court/commander_gareth_ro_enheart.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

**Commander Gareth Ro-enheart** was the renowned Head of the Royal Guard, hero
of the Sunken Citadel, and father to Elijah Ro-enheart. His unexplained
disappearance under sealed royal orders following Elijah's 10th birthday forms
the primary personal catalyst driving Elijah's journey across Sacyra.

== 1. Identity & Lineage

* **Full Name**: Commander Gareth Ro-enheart
* **Title**: Former Head of the Sacyran Royal Guard; Knight-Commander of Sacyra
* **Kinship**:
  - **Son**: Elijah Ro-enheart (Cadet Vanguard, bearer of the Shield of Lux)
  - **Mother**: Magolia Ro-enheart ("Grandma Magolia", matriarch of Dio Village)
* **Status**: Missing in Action / Sealed Royal Dispatch (Presumed Alive)

== 2. Legend & Martial Legacy

Gareth Ro-enheart is remembered throughout Sacyra as an incorruptible paragon
of chivalric virtue. Decades prior, when an allied barbarian host breached the
frontier palisades, Commander Gareth anchored the Sunken Citadel for seven days
without relief, personally deflecting siege bolts with his reinforced tower shield.

He passed this philosophy to his young son Elijah:
[quote, Gareth Ro-enheart]
"A shield is not a wall to hide behind, but a promise made to the people standing
behind you."

== 3. The 10th Birthday Vanishing & Royal Intrigue

On the morning following Elijah's **10th birthday**, Commander Gareth received
a direct royal summons from King Jihad and departed the capital with an elite
retinue for the southern frontier. Neither he nor his men ever returned.

* **Official Crown Statement**: Gareth was lost during a classified border survey.
* **The Reality**: Clues uncovered by Elijah and Katrina reveal that Gareth
  discovered King Jihad's clandestine expeditions to unearth the Abyssal Vaults.
  Refusing to participate in forbidden dark alchemy, Gareth was either imprisoned
  in a subterranean redoubt or went into deep exile to protect the realm.

== Chapter 30: Threat Dossier: The Dark Lord

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/threats/the_dark_lord.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

In the authentic 2017–2018 demo script and canonical narrative architecture, the ultimate
antagonist is designated strictly as **The Dark Lord**. He is not a generic cardboard demon,
but the tragic culmination of three millennia of festering bitterness born from the catastrophe
of the 11th Festival: the ancient Libiran youth **Usuru**.

== 1. Ground-Truth Canonical Identity & Origin

* **The Nameless Wanderer of Libira:** Born on the remote southern frontier of Libira during
  the First King's reign, he grew up as a restless three-day wanderer whose sole enduring devotion
  was his annual week-long pilgrimage to the Capital for the Festival of Peoples.
* **The 11th Year Rupture:** Seeking an audience with the sovereign during the rumored "plague,"
  the eleven-year-old boy discovered the First King's mummified corpse and Prince Mi-ren's
  protective projection ritual. Viewing the vigil as a monstrous deception, his terror triggered
  an accidental collision with the ritual array.
* **The Birth of Tenebris:** The catastrophic collapse of the boy's innocent faith produced an
  anomalous metaphysical surge that birthed raw **Tenebris** into reality. Taking the name
  **Usuru**, he rallied Libira and border kingdoms to wage the 7-Year Great War of annexation.
* **The 3,000-Year Descent:** Following his armistice with Prince Mi-ren under the Accord of Kings,
  Usuru refused spiritual reconciliation. He retreated into the subterranean abyssal vaults, where
  his unresolved grief and fury curdled over three thousand years until his mortal name was lost
  to history, remembered only with terror as **The Dark Lord**.

== 2. Narrative Function: The Overarching "A Plot"

* **Existential Backdrop:** The Dark Lord is not an early-game hallway encounter. He represents
  the overarching existential shadow ("The A Plot") looming over Sacyra.
* **Foreground vs. Background:** While provincial survival (Dio Village), noble exploitation
  (St. Baron), and King Jihad's treason form the active foreground ("B Plot" and "C Plot"),
  the awakening of The Dark Lord informs the dread of the entire continent.
* **The Metaphysical Siege Window:** The Dark Lord strategically aligns his vanguard strikes
  with the annual **Festival of Heroes**, knowing that the boundary to the Dreamscape thins to
  gossamer during that week, providing a planar bridge to reclaim the surface world.

== 3. "The King's Curse" & Possession of King Jihad

* **The Planar Rift:** When Usuru shattered the First King's stasis array 3,000 years ago, he left
  a jagged planar wound directly beneath the dias of the High Throne.
* **Psychic Conduit:** For three millennia, sovereigns were exposed to ethereal backwash. When
  King Jihad attempted to harness void power to usurp the Dark Lord, Jihad's megalomania inverted
  the conduit, allowing a dormant remnant of Usuru's consciousness to possess the reigning monarch.
* **Two-Half Campaign Resolution:** In Act I, heroes confront the possessed King Jihad; in Act II,
  they must travel into the deep abyssal threshold to face The Dark Lord himself.

== 4. Metaphysical Neutrality & True Resolution

* **Not Elemental Evil:** Darkness (Tenebris) is a cosmic apex force of rest, mystery, and severance.
  The Dark Lord represents Tenebris corrupted by an unhealed heart of vengeance.
* **The Ugly Truth:** Defeating The Dark Lord through violence alone (Neutral Ending) leaves the First
  King marooned and the cycle dormant. Only by confronting "The Ugly Truth"—acknowledging Usuru's
  valid childhood pain and achieving the **Stance of Equilibrium**—can heroes permanently release
  Usuru's tortured soul and heal the realm.

== Chapter 31: Threat Dossier: Dream Eaters

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/lore/threats/dream_eaters.adoc`

== 1. Astral Parasites of the Slumber Realm

**Dream Eaters** are shifting metaphysical entities native to the borders of The Dreamscape.

* **Nature**: Astral parasites that feed on unresolved regrets, fears, and psychic vulnerabilities.
* **Combat Role**:
  - Manifesting during Chrono's trial, they serve as the opening combat challenge in the game.
  - **Base Vigor**: $70$ Threat HP.
  - **Defensive Attributes**: $11$ Physical Defense (DEF), $8$ Magic Defense (MDF).
  - **Flank Vigor**: $70$ HP representing flanking swarms susceptible to *Cuttatio Tenebris* splash damage.
* **Significance**: Defeating the Dream Eaters demonstrates the bearer's ability to maintain mental fortitude and twin-relic resonance, breaking free of the dream's illusions.

= Volume III: Ludology & Tactical TRPG Combat Mechanics

Grid-based tactical warfare, relic stance machines, terrain modifiers, continuous invariant testing, and game design curation.

== Chapter 32: Dual-Layer TRPG Combat Architecture

*Source File*: `/home/professorseanex/Tenebris and Lux/docs/game_design/tactical_trpg_system.adoc`

[abstract]
== Overview

This document specifies the dual-layer combat architecture of *Tenebris and Lux*:
a **Tactical Strategy RPG (TRPG)** featuring a **grid-based tactical battlefield** coupled with
**cinematic traditional side-scroll / action-based combat resolution**. Conceived during the 2014–2018
RPG Maker genesis of CreativeWorkzStudio, this design bridges grand tactical positioning with visceral,
action-driven 2D engagement.

== 1. Dual-Layer Battle Architecture

The combat engine operates across two seamless, interconnected operational loops:

```text
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                   PHASE 1: TACTICAL GRID PHASE                         │
│  Tile Movement, Line-of-Sight, Terrain Defenses, Flanking & Squad ZOC  │
└───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                                    │ Target Engagement
                                    ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│              PHASE 2: CINEMATIC COMBAT RESOLUTION (SIDE-VIEW)          │
│  2D Action Clashes, Timed Wards, Severance Slashes & Stance Techniques │
└───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                                    │ Resolution & Damage Sync
                                    ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                  PERSISTENT SIMULATION STATE UPDATE                    │
│   HP Deductions, Stagger States, Relic Resonance & Moral Sway Deltas   │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

== 2. Phase 1: Grid-Based Tactical Battlefield

The overarching strategic layer takes place upon a discrete 2D grid map representing the castles,
frontier passes, and battlegrounds of the **Kingdom of Sacyra**.

=== 1. Grid Topography & Spatial Invariants

* **Coordinate Space**: Discrete integer grid `(grid_x, grid_y)`.
* **Unit Footprint**: Standard units occupy a $1 \times 1$ grid tile; major boss constructs
  (such as The Dark Lord's Siege Automata) occupy $2 \times 2$ or $3 \times 3$ footprints.
* **Terrain Types & Modifiers**:
  - **Stone Flagstones / Road**: $1.0\times$ movement cost, $+0\%$ evasion.
  - **Ramparts / Elevated Battlement**: $1.5\times$ movement cost, $+20\%$ physical defense,
    $+1$ attack range for ranged skills.
  - **Frontier Thicket / Rubble**: $2.0\times$ movement cost, $+15\%$ evasion, blocks cavalry charge.
  - **Void Mire (Abyssal Blight)**: $2.5\times$ movement cost, inflicts $-5$ HP decay
    per turn on non-void units.

=== 2. Movement & Zone of Control (ZOC)

* **Movement Budget**: Units possess a base `movement_tiles` statistic (e.g. Katrina: 6 tiles; Elijah: 4 tiles in heavy armor).
* **Zone of Control**: Moving into an adjacent tile directly forward of a hostile soldier halts further movement for that turn, anchoring frontline formations.
* **Directional Facing**: Units finish their turn facing one of four cardinal directions (`NORTH`, `SOUTH`, `EAST`, `WEST`). Attacking an enemy from their flank grants $+15\%$ critical chance; attacking from the rear guarantees an unguardable strike.

=== 3. Turn Sequencing

* **Dynamic Initiative Order**: Turn execution is governed by unit Agility. Fast skirmishers (like Katrina) act earlier in the round, while heavy vanguard armorers act later with higher defensive resilience.

== 3. Phase 2: Cinematic Combat Resolution (Side-View Action)

When an attacker initiates an assault or when two opposing units clash on the grid, the game seamlessly
transitions into a dedicated **2D Side-View Cinematic Battle Scene**.

=== 1. Visual & Staging Presentation

* The attacker approaches from the left (player units) or right (enemy units) across a dynamic 2D battlefield backdrop matching the current terrain (e.g., Sunken Citadel battlements, ash-strewn frontier).
* Character sprites render with expressive combat animations: Idle combat stance, forward dash, heavy strike, shield block, recoil, stagger, and victory flourishes.

=== 2. Combat Mechanics During Cinematic Resolution

1. **Active Input & Timed Agency**:
   - Rather than passive numbers scrolling past, combat resolution incorporates timed player inputs:
     - **Timed Ward (Lux Guard)**: Pressing the ward button precisely upon enemy weapon contact triggers a **Perfect Deflection**, absorbing 100% of incoming damage and staggering the foe.
     - **Twilight Severance (Tenebris Strike)**: Pressing strike during the forward dash activates a **Deep Cleave**, bypassing a percentage of enemy armor.
2. **Stagger & Poise Meter**:
   - Every battler possesses a **Poise / Stagger Gauge** ($0 \dots 100$).
   - Repeated heavy strikes or shield bashes deplete enemy poise. When poise hits zero, the target enters **Staggered Vulnerability**, leaving them defenseless against critical finishers.
3. **Stance Animation Morphing**:
   - The character's active stance directly dictates their cinematic combat posture and move set:
     - `STANCE_WARDING`: Elijah locks the Shield of Lux into the earth; golden starlight forms a kinetic buckler.
     - `STANCE_STRIKING`: Katrina or Elijah lowers their center of gravity, dragging the Sword of Tenebris as twilight embers trail the blade.
     - `STANCE_EQUILIBRIUM`: Fluid parry-and-counter sequencing utilizing both armaments in rapid harmony.

== 4. Dual-Protagonist Synergy: Elijah & Katrina

The tactical depth of *Tenebris and Lux* emerges from combining the distinct capabilities of Elijah and Katrina:

=== Elijah Ro-enheart (Frontline Guardian)

[cols="1,3", options="header"]
|===
| Feature | Tactical Grid & Cinematic Specification
| **Unit Class** | Royal Vanguard / Aegis Knight
| **Grid Role** | Anchors the defensive perimeter; draws enemy agro; intercepts projectiles.
| **Special Trait: Royal Intercept** | If an adjacent ally (within 1 tile) is targeted by an attack, Elijah leaps in front to receive the blow, initiating the cinematic defense in their stead.
| **Relic Mastery** | **Shield of Lux**: Projects a 2-tile radius **Sanctuary Aura** on the grid that grants allies $+25\%$ physical defense and immunity to void blight.
| **Cinematic Moves** | *Aegis Slam*, *Radiant Shockwave*, *Bastion Guard*, *Solar Flare Riposte*.
|===

=== Katrina Jaren (Tactical Skirmisher)

[cols="1,3", options="header"]
|===
| Feature | Tactical Grid & Cinematic Specification
| **Unit Class** | Guild Outrider / Twilight Blade
| **Grid Role** | Flanks heavy units; assassinates backline spellcasters; disarms siege engines.
| **Special Trait: Guild Acrobatics** | Can move through tiles occupied by enemy infantry without triggering Zone-of-Control lock.
| **Relic Mastery** | **Sword of Tenebris**: Can activate **Shadow Walk**, cloaking in mist on the grid to become untargetable by ranged attacks until her next strike.
| **Cinematic Moves** | *Abyssal Dash*, *Crescent Sever*, *Shadow Dance*, *Mist-Veil Feint*.
|===

=== Tandem Linked Combat (The Duo Duel)

When Elijah and Katrina are positioned on adjacent grid tiles and either initiates combat, both heroes
enter the cinematic side-view battle scene together:

* **Shield & Sword Flank**: Elijah executes an *Aegis Slam* that knocks the enemy backward, launching them into Katrina's airborne *Crescent Sever*.
* **Harmonized Equilibrium**: Both heroes combine the radiance of Lux and the edge of Tenebris, unleashing a dual finishing art that sweeps across multiple targets.

== 5. Persistent State & Moral Sway Feedback

Upon completion of the cinematic resolution:

1. **HP & Wound Conservation**: Remaining unit health updates onto the tactical grid. Fallen units are removed or enter down-but-not-dead states.
